Matters relating to creditor and the burrower (sec 36)
a. trustee is entitled to carry out, valuation of the company's assets, project analysis or management analysis,
b. period of repayment to debenture holder, interest rate , mode of repayment, and matters of conversion(if there is such provision)
c. provision on rights of other creditors over the assets and liabilities that may arise there
d. in case of violation of agreement, trustee may
    - Take possession of assets or properties of such company or,
    - property taken as the security or guarantee or,
    - Hold the security of guarantee
    - sell
e. Procedures for payment of the service charge and other of debenture trustee
f. trustee shall not be liable to any loss from any act done  by the trustee in that capacity,
g. in occurrence of liquidation trustee is entitled to take such legal action in behalf of the debenture-holder and exercise powers of the debenture holder
h. other necessary matters

objective
- Ensure assets of the company are sufficient to discharge the debenture
- ensure prospectus is consistent with terms of debenture
- ensure Co. does not breach debenture issue deed and take steps for remedy if there are any breach
- entertain the grievance of debenture holders
- call meeting of debenture holders as and when necessary

Disqualification
- owns shares in the Co.
- receive money
- given any guarantee on behalf of Co.

- power of calculation of property of assets to be taken

1st issue right shares
(pre-emption right ) -> right to existing SH to buy new share before offered to others
    -> send notice for issue 15d prior -> at least 3 times consecutively 
    -> 35d time limit should be given to subscribe right shares 

then issue unsubscribed shares to general public as IPO ^PVLwgyIU

Benefits of right shares
- more control on Co. by existing SH
- helpful to increase the goodwill of Co.
- no cost of issuing shares to public (advertising cost)
- capital formation 
- more scientific -> not all share will get by single SH , it will be in proportion of existing shares ^BNHlw1yy

Types of PS
1. Cumulative and Non-cumulative preference shares
→ dividend goes on accumulating till it is fully paid off
2. Participating and Non-participating preference shares
→ participating preference shares have one or both of the following rights
    - To participate further in the profits either along with, or after 
        payment of a certain rate of dividends on equity shares.
    - To participate in the surplus assets at the time of winding up
3. Convertible and non-convertible preference share
4. Redeemable and irredeemable preference shares ^H8dH2cpQ

Preference share
Prospectus ^axGNXYEA

1. Whether preference is given while paying dividends
2. Percentage of dividends receivable 
3. cumulative or non-cumulative 
4. Whether preference is given while paying during liquidation
5. Whether voting right is attached in the case of 
    preference share or also in other matters
    & the portion to which such right is exercisable
6. convertible or not
7. redeemable or irredeemable
8. whether premium is payable on redemption ^4mKzUd5K
